Implement `_adam_z(z, beta, max_iters, deadline, lr0=0.1, verbose=False)`:
```
m=np.zeros_like(z); v=np.zeros_like(z); t=0; best_z=z.copy(); best_val=inf; patience=0
for it in range(max_iters):
 if time>deadline: break

   if val_trial <= val + 1e-12:
      accept; break
   scale*=0.5

 ...

```

Let's refine `_lse_score_grad_z`:
```
def _lse_score_grad_z(z, beta):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = z.size
    z = z - np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(z)
    a = e / e.sum()
    N = _next_pow2(2*n-1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, N)[:2*n-1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    d = n * c
    dmax = d.max()
    parasitic: if beta <=0 use max? We'll use positive.
    s = beta*(d-dmax); s = np.clip(s, -400, 400)
    exps = np.exp(s); sume = exps.sum(); w = exps / sume
    L = dmax + np.log(sume)/beta
    score = 2*L
    # R = Bw
    w_rev = w[::-1]
    N3 = _next_pow2(3*n-2)
    AR3 = np.fft.rfft(a, N3)
    WR3 = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, N3)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(AR3*WR3, N3)
    R = conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    # score=2*L=2*sum w d. grad score wrt a = 4n R? Since d=n c, L=∑w d (up to derivative? ∂L=∑w ∂d? Actually L = cmax + logsumexp; derivative =∑w ∂d? because d values. ∂d_i=n*2R_i? Wait R defined ∑a_j w_{i+j}. derivative of c wrt a_i=2 a_{k-i} sum w =2R. d=n c => derivative n*2R. L derivative=∑w? Actually L already weighted sum of d; ∂L=∑w ∂d =2n R. score=2L -> 4n R.
    grad_a = 4.0 * n * R
    # project to log tangent
    mu = float(a.dot(grad_a))
    grad_z = grad_a - a * mu
    # Since softmax z invariant to constant, gradient sum zero.
    return float(score), np.nan_to_num(grad_z)
```

Yes because dmax derivative term: derivative dmax is active; subtract * careful: if dmax is one of terms, L = max + (1/β)log∑ e^{β(d-dmax)}. Let u=d-dmax. derivative dmax term 1*δ_{maxd} plus (∑w ∂u). ∂u_i=∂d_i - δ_i. Sum w ∂u =∑w∂d -1. So total =∑w∂d. Good.

But our R computation corresponds to ∑_j a_j w_{i+j}. Yes ∂c_i =2R. Good.

For high β, active peak maybe k center, R_i = a_{k-i}, max up to edge ~0.01, grad_a=404 for edge, 0 for others; mu=a·grad ~ maybe 1? grad_z edge ~404 - a*... ~404, large. L-BFGS may step small. **Adam Z implementation**
```
def _adam_z(z0, beta, deadline, max_iters, lr=0.05, beta1=0.9, beta2=0.999, zmin=-40, zmax=40):
    z = z0.copy(); m=...; v=...; best_z=z.copy(); best_val=inf; bad=0
    t=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() + 0.01 > deadline: break
       val, grad = _lse_score_grad_z(z,beta)
       if not finite: break
       if val < best_val: best_val=val; best_z=z.copy(); bad=0
       else: bad+=1
       if bad > 500 and lr>1e-8: lr*=0.5; bad=0 # maybe
       t+=1
       m=...; v=...
       step = lr * m/(sqrt(v)+1e-8)
       # scale step to max norm maybe 0.2
       sn=np.linalg.norm(step)
       if sn>0.2: step*=0.2/sn
       z = np.clip(z - step, zmin,zmax)
    # Also exact evaluate best_z and z
```
Since objective value smooth, best smooth may not exact; final exact. Could use exact periodically to update exact best. Use `_exact_fast(z)` every 100 iter.
- Adam can run many iterations; setting max_iters = int(remaining*200?) But we check time. It may spend all time; allocate explicit.
- For high β, Adam may oscillate; use lower lr 0.01.

**Active peak moves**:
A simple targeted move: For an active peak k, coefficient d_k = n ∑ a_i a_{k-i}. To reduce it while keeping sum, we can shift mass from middle terms to edges? For k near center, terms involve pairs around k/2. We can identify pairs (i,j) with i+j=k. The coefficient is sum over all i in valid. If we decrease a_i and increase a_j? The effect on many peaks. Hard.

Coordinate descent pair move (i -> j):
- For normalized a, moving δ from i to j: a_i' = a_i-δ, a_j'=a_j+δ. Coefficient c_k changes: Δc_k = 2δ (a_{k-j} + a_{k-i}) - 2δ^2? Let's derive p'=p+δ(x^j-x^i). p'^2 = p^2 + 2δ p(x^j-x^i) + δ^2(x^j-x^i)^2.
Coefficient k: linear term 2δ( p_{k-j} +? coefficient of x^k in p x^j is a_{k-j}; in -p x^i is -a_{k-i}; so 2δ(a_{k-j}-a_{k-i}). Wait check indices: p x^j coeff k = a_{k-j}; p x^i: a_{k-i}. So Δlinear=2δ(a_{k-j}-a_{k-i}). Quadratic: δ^2[x^{2j}+x^{2i}-2x^{i+j}] coefficient at k: δ^2(δ_{k,2j}+δ_{k,2i}-2δ_{k,i+j}). For 0≤δ≤a_i. This is a quadratic for each k. We can choose δ that minimizes max over active peaks approximately. This is a 1D convex? max of quadratics (some convex, some concave depending signs) not necessarily. But we can evaluate exact for candidate δs (0, min(0.1a_i, ...), etc.) and accept if improves. Random pair moves can improve.

Implement `_pair_move_polish(a, deadline, trials)`:
- Use current a normalized.
- Use active peaks to choose i? Moving mass from high a_i that contributes to active peaks to low a_j that doesn't.
- For random trials: pick i with high a_i and peak contribution? We can compute for current top peaks K an array `contrib_i = ∑_{k∈K} w_k a_{k-i}` (R). High R_i? Gradient direction: moving mass from high R_i to low R_j reduces Q. We can choose i in top quantile of R, j in bottom quantile with a_j>0. Try δ = fraction of a_i (e.g. 0.01,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5). Evaluate exact fast for sequence after move O(n log n), maybe 100 trials*5ms=0.5s. Could help. But full optimization already moves like this.

Let's define `_optimize_start(a0, deadline, level_n, tag, use_parent=None)`:
This is main optimizer for one starting sequence length n. It should:
1. Ensure a normalized, z=log(a+1e-15). Keep best exact.
2. If use_parent not None and same length: blend initial maybe.
3. Run β stages with time split. For each stage, use L-BFGS and Adam.
4. After β stages, run exact subgradient/pair moves until deadline.
5. Return best exact, a.

Need not allow level to overspend; estimates.

Detailed `_optimize_start`:
```
def _optimize_start(a0, deadline, tag=""):
    a = normalize(a0); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_fast(a); print
    z = log(a+eps)
    # Beta schedule based on n and remaining
    if n <= 1024: betas=[2,10,50,200,1000,5000,20000,100000]
    elif n <= 2048: betas=[2,10,50,200,1000,5000,20000]
    elif n <= 4096: betas=[2,10,50,200,1000,5000]
    elif n <= 8192: betas=[2,10,50,200,1000]
    else: betas=[2,10,50,200,1000]
    # time weights maybe lower for low beta: [1,2,4,6,8,10,12,14]?
    weights = [1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8, 10, 12][:len(betas)]
    rem_total = deadline - now - 30 (reserve polish)
    for beta, wgt in zip:
       t_sub = now + rem_total*wgt/sum
       if t_sub < now+10: continue
       # L-BFGS
       z, best = _lbfgs_z(z,beta,t_sub*0.6, maxfun_cap based n)
       a=softmax(z); update
       # Adam if time
       z = _adam_z(z,beta, t_sub*0.92, max_iters=int(rem*100), lr=...)
       a=softmax(z); update
    # polynomial exact subgradient/nesterov on a best
    rem=deadline-now
    if rem>15: a,best_sc = _exact_subgrad_polish(a, rem-5, tag)
    return best_sc,best_a
```

```
def _exact_polish(a0, deadline):
  a=normalize(a0); best=a; best_sc=exact_fast(a)
  it=0; step=0.05
  while time<deadline:
     d, dmax, c = compute
     active = top indices where d>= dmax - tol
     # use top k weights from softmax active with β=50 on (d-dmax) among active? Or uniform top len.
     k = min(len(active), max(2, int(0.001*n))) maybe 10
     w = softmax(beta_act*(d[active]-dmax)) uniform?
     R = Bw (using FFT or direct if k small). Q=d.dot(w). g=R-Q
     gnorm=norm(g); if small break
     dir = g/gnorm
     # try step sizes [step,0.5step,0.25...]
     best_local_sc=best_sc
     for s in [step, step/2, step/4, step/8]:
        cand = a - s*dir
        cand=clip(cand,0,None); normalize
        sc=exact_fast(cand)
        if sc<best_local_sc: accept; break
     if improved: best...; step = min(1.5*step, 0.2)
     else: step*=0.5; if step<1e-6 break
  return best
```

**Accuracy of gradient in z for L-BFGS**:
Derivative of softmax: If z updated, a_i = e_i/Z. ∂a_i/∂z_j = a_i(δ_ij - a_j). Objective F(a). ∂F/∂z_j = ∑_i ∂F/∂a_i ∂a_i/∂z_j = ∑_i f_i a_i(δ_ij - a_j) = a_j f_j - a_j∑a_i f_i = a_j(f_j - μ). Equivalent to f - a μ (since f_i - a_i μ). Yes.
But in code `grad_z=grad_a - a*mu`. Good.

For smooth LSE value=2L, grad_a=4n R. Does ∑a grad_a =? ∑a 4nR =4n Q? Q=∑w d=∑a_i R_i? Since d=n c, ∑a_iR_i=∑ w n c = nQ? Wait R_i=∑ a_j w_{i+j}. ∑_i a_i R_i=∑_{i,j} a_i a_j w_{i+j}=∑_k w_k c_k =: Q_c. d=n c, ∑ w d = n Q_c. Let D=∑w d. Then ∑a_i (2nR_i)=2n Q_c =2D. grad_a=4n R, sum a grad =4n Q_c=2D? Actually 4nQ_c. No matter. Subtracting mu makes tangent.

But note `R` computed using w over d coefficients (length c). w normalized sum 1. ∑a_iR_i=Q_c. OK.

Low β gradient using uniform w: R_i=∑ a_j/(2n-1) ~1/(2n). grad_a~4n/(2n)=2; after z projection ~2. Good.

**Potential performance bottleneck**: In `_lse_score_grad_z`, computing `w_rev = w[::-1]`, FFT of w_rev length N3 each call. N3 for n=10240 = next_pow2(30718)=32768? Wait 3n-2=30718, next_pow2=32768. For self conv N=next_pow2(20479)=32768. Same N! Great. For n=10240, N3=32768. For n=16384, 3n=49150, N3=65536; self N=32768? 2n-1=32767, N=32768. So two FFT lengths maybe same for 10240. For n=16384, self N=32768 and cross N=65536. OK.

```
if N_cross == N_self:
   AR = A_self
else: AR = rfft(a,N_cross)
WR = rfft(w_rev,N_cross)
R=irfft(AR*WR,N_cross)
```
This saves one FFT for n=10240. Good.

`_adam_z` implementation in detail:
```
def _adam_z(z0,beta,deadline,max_step=0.3,lr=0.05,beta1=0.9,beta2=0.999,eval_exact_every=200):
    z = np.asarray(z0).copy()
    m=np.zeros_like(z); v=np.zeros_like(z); t=0
    best_smooth_z=z.copy(); best_smooth=inf
    best_exact_z=z.copy(); best_exact=_exact_fast_from_z(z)
    zmin=-45; zmax=45
    no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1:
        score, grad = _lse_score_grad_z(z,beta)
        if not finite break
        if score < best_smooth - 1e-12:
            best_smooth=score; best_smooth_z=z.copy(); no_improve=0
        else:
            no_improve +=1
            if no_improve>300 and lr>1e-6:
                lr*=0.5; no_improve=0
        t+=1
        mb=beta1*m+(1-beta1)*grad
        vb=beta2*v+(1-beta2)*grad*grad
        step = lr * mb/(np.sqrt(vb)+1e-8)
        sn=np.linalg.norm(step)
        if sn>max_step:
            step *= max_step/sn
        z = np.clip(z - step, zmin, zmax)
        if t % eval_exact_every == 0:
            sc=_exact_fast_from_z(z)
            if sc<best_exact:
                best_exact=sc; best_exact_z=z.copy()
    # final exact
    scZ=_exact_fast_from_z(z); if scZ<best_exact: best_exact_z=z.copy()
    scS=_exact_fast_from_z(best_smooth_z); if scS<best_exact: best_exact_z=best_smooth_z
    return best_exact_z, best_exact
```
But if best_exact_z differs from current z, momentum lost. We could return current z (last) not best exact for chain? But we want best. For chain, maybe use current z because smoother? Yet if exact best intermediate, use it. Let's return `best_exact_z` and score. The caller can set z=log(best_exact? no) to continue. Maybe returning best exact is good.
- If `eval_exact_every` exact calls use FFT, each exact only self conv (one rfft+irfft) cheaper. OK.
- `lr` base maybe adapt to n? For n=10240, lr=0.05 max_step=0.25. For small n, lr=0.1. Parameter.

```
def _structured_inits(n, rng, parent, cap=35):
    inits=[]
    def? no. We'll manually add.
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    # parent
    if parent is not None:
       arr=_normalize(_resample(parent,n)); inits.append(arr)
       arr=_normalize(arr[::-1]); inits.append(arr)
       # edge blend parent
       for edge_alpha in [1,5,20]:
          edge=np.exp(-x/0.02)+np.exp(-(1-x)/0.02)
          arr=_normalize(arr*(1+edge_alpha*edge/edge.max())); inits.append
    inits.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    for p in [0.1,0.2,0.3,0.45,0.6,0.8,1.0]:
       xx=np.linspace(1e-6,1-1e-6,n)
       arr=xx**(-p)*(1-xx)**(-p)+1e-5
       arr=_normalize(np.clip(arr,0,1e6)); inits.append
    for alpha in [0.5,1,2,5]:
       arr=1+alpha*(2*x-1)**2; inits
       arr=1+alpha*np.abs(2*x-1); inits
       arr=1+alpha*(1-np.abs(2*x-1)); inits # peak center
    for scale in [0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1]:
       edge=np.exp(-x/max(scale,1e-6))+np.exp(-(1-x)/max(scale,1e-6))
       for alpha in [1,3,10,30]:
          arr=1+alpha*edge; inits
    # cosine
    for f in [1,2,3,4]:
      for amp in [0.5,1.5,3.0]:
        arr=1+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x); if min>0? clip anyway
        inits.append(_normalize(np.clip(arr,0,1))) if any
        arr=1+amp*np.cos(np.pi*f*x); similar
    # random smooth
    N=_next_pow2(n)
    for _ in range(5):
       freqs=np.fft.rfftfreq(N)
       power=1/((n*np.abs(freqs)+1)**float(rng.uniform(1,3)))
       power[0]=0
       phase=rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi,size=len(power))
       noise=np.fft.irfft(power*np.exp(1j*phase), N)[:n]
       arr=1+float(rng.uniform(0.2,1.0))*noise
       arr=_normalize(np.clip(arr,0,1)); inits.append
    # pure random
    for _ in range(3):
       arr=0.01+0.99*rng.random(n); inits
    # Filter unique? Could append up to cap.
    # Return first cap maybe but maintain diversity. We can skip duplicates by comparing first few? Not necessary.
    if len(inits)>cap: inits=inits[:cap]
    return [arr for arr in inits if arr.size==n and np.isfinite(arr).all()]
```
Need ensure `np.exp(1j*phase)` works; yes.
For p=1.0, xx^-2 huge endpoints maybe overflow? xx min 1e-6, xx^-2=1e12, product=1e24, finite. Normalize. OK.
`np.cos(np.pi*f*x)` may be negative; clip.

```
def _perturb_a(a, rng, strength=None):
   a=normalize(a); n=a.size
   if strength is None: strength=rng.uniform(0.2,0.5)
   mode=int(rng.integers(0,5))
   if mode0: q=a*np.exp(strength*rng.standard_normal(n))
   elif1: target edge: x linspace; q=a*(1+strength*(exp(-x/0.02)+...))
   elif2: q=(1-t)a + t/range? blend with uniform/edge
   elif3: smooth noise
   else: random swaps/mult
   return normalize(clip(q))
```

Let's detail `_optimize_start` implementation with time phases.

```
def _optimize_start(a0, deadline, tag=""):
    a = _normalize(np.asarray(a0,dtype=float).ravel())
    best_a = a.copy(); best_sc = _exact_fast(a)
    z = _logseq(a)
    n=z.size
    # If deadline too close, return
    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: return best_sc,best_a
    # reserve last 10% for exact/adampolish? Use 0.85 for smoother
    smooth_dead = deadline - max(5.0, 0.08*(deadline-time.monotonic()))
    # beta schedule and weights
    if n <= 512:
        betas=[2,10,50,200,1000,5000,20000,100000]
        weights=[1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8]
        maxfun_cap=30000
        adam_lr=0.1; max_step=0.5
    elif n <= 1024:
        betas=[2,10,50,200,1000,5000,20000,100000]
        weights=[1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8]
        maxfun_cap=15000; lr=.08
    elif n <= 2048:
        betas=[2,10,50,200,1000,5000,20000]
        weights=[1,2,3,4,5,6,7]; cap=8000
    elif n <= 4096:
        betas=[2,10,50,200,1000,5000]
        weights=[1,2,3,4,5,6]; cap=5000
    else:
        betas=[2,10,50,200,1000,5000] maybe add 20000 if n<=8192? For 10240 include up to 5000; maybe add 20000 if time.
        if n <= 8192: betas=[2,10,50,200,1000,5000,20000]; weights...
        else betas=[2,10,50,200,1000,5000]
    smooth_total = smooth_dead - now
    sumw=sum(weights)
    for i,beta in enumerate(betas):
       stage_time = smooth_total * weights[i]/sumw
       if stage_time < 8: continue
       sub_dead = now + stage_time
       # lbfgs
       lb_dead = now + stage_time*0.55
       if time.monotonic() < lb_dead-1:
          z, val = _lbfgs_z(z,beta,lb_dead,maxfun_cap)
       # update from z
       a = _softmax(z)
       sc = _exact_fast(a)
       if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
       # Adam
       adam_dead = sub_dead - 1
       if time.monotonic() < adam_dead-2:
          z, sc_z = _adam_z(z, beta, adam_dead, max_step=..., lr=...)
          a = _softmax(z); sc=_exact_fast(a); update
          # also evaluate @best_a? no
    # final exact polish: use high beta Adam without L-BFGS if time
    rem=deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 10:
       beta_pol = 20000 if n <=4096 else 10000
       z = _logseq(best_a)
       z, sc = _adam_z(z, beta_pol, deadline, max_step=0.2, lr=0.02)
       a=softmax(z); sc exact update
    return best_sc,best_a
```

Detailed functions:

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()

def _normalize(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0: return np.ones(1)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(arr.size)
    return arr / s
```
No nested.

`_self_conv_fft`:
```
def _self_conv_fft(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    M=2*n-1
    N=_next_pow2(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    out=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:M]
    # clean
    out=np.real(out)
    out[np.asarray(out)<0] = 0.0 # cannot assign? out = np.maximum(out,0.0)
    return np.maximum(out,0.0)
```

`_exact_fast(a)`:
```
def _exact_fast(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=arr.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    arr=np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,None)
    S=float(arr.sum())
    if S<1e-12: return np.inf
    c=_self_conv_fft(arr)
    if c.size==0: return np.inf
    val=2.0*float(n)*float(c.max())/(S*S)
    return float(val) if np.isfinite(val) else np.inf
```

`_resample`:
```
def _resample(a,n):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel(); a=np.nan_to_num(a,nan=0.0,posinf=1e6,neginf=0.0); m=a.size
   if m==0: return np.ones(n)
   if m==n: return a.copy()
   if n==1: return np.array([float(a.sum())])
   xo=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m); xn=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   return np.interp(xn,xo,a).astype(np.float64)
```

`_softmax`:
```
def _softmax(z):
   z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if z.size==0: return np.empty(0)
   z=z-np.max(z)
   e=np.exp(z)
   s=float(e.sum())
   if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.ones(z.size)/z.size
   return e/s
```

`_logseq`:
```
def _logseq(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if a.size==0: return np.empty(0)
   a=np.nan_to_num(a,nan=0.0,posinf=1e6,neginf=0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,1e-300,None)
   return np.log(a)
```

`_lse_grad_a`:
```
def _lse_grad_a(a,beta):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf,np.zeros(0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    # Use scaled to sum 1? Score invariant; for gradient with sum not 1, need S factors. Better normalize internally.
    if S<1e-15: return np.inf, np.zeros(n)
    a_norm = a/S
    c=_self_conv_fft(a_norm)
    d=n*c
    dmax=float(d.max())
    if not np.isfinite(dmax): return np.inf,np.zeros(n)
    beta=float(beta)
    if beta <= 0:
        # use max subgradient? not used
        beta=1.0
    s=beta*(d-dmax)
    s=np.clip(s,-400.0,400.0)
    e=np.exp(s)
    sume=float(e.sum())
    if sume<=0 or not np.isfinite(sume):
        w=np.full(2*n-1, 1.0/(2*n-1))
        L=dmax
    else:
        w=e/sume
        L=dmax + np.log(sume)/beta
    # R
    w_rev=w[::-1]
    Nself=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    Ncross=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a_norm,Nself)
    if Ncross==Nself:
        AR=A
    else:
        AR=np.fft.rfft(a_norm,Ncross)
    WR=np.fft.rfft(w_rev,Ncross)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(AR*WR,Ncross)
    seg=conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    R=np.nan_to_num(seg,nan=0.0,posinf=0.0,neginf=0.0)
    # score smooth value for normalized a (sum1)
    score=2.0*float(L)
    grad_a=4.0*float(n)*R
    # if original a not normalized, gradient wrt original? We only call with normalized. OK.
    return float(score), grad_a
```
Is LSE score using d=n*c; max d. Score=2*max d at β∞, which equals 2*n*max c = objective. Good.
If beta <=0, not.

`_lse_score_grad_z`:
```
def _lse_score_grad_z(z,beta):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=_softmax(z)
    score, grad_a = _lse_grad_a(a,beta)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(z)
    mu=float(a.dot(grad_a))
    grad_z=grad_a - a*mu
    grad_z=np.nan_to_num(grad_z,nan=0.0,posinf=0.0,neginf=0.0)
    return float(score), grad_z
```

`_exact_fast_z`:
```
def _exact_fast_z(z):
    a=_softmax(z)
    return _exact_fast(a)
```

`_lbfgs_z`:
No lambdas. Need `minimize` with `jac=True` and fun returns tuple. However SciPy's L-BFGS-B with `jac=True` expects fun to return tuple? Documentation: `fun : callable, Other callables that also return tuple (f, g)` yes.
```
def _lbfgs_z(z0,beta,deadline,maxfun_cap=3000):
    z=np.asarray(z0,dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if z.size==0: return z, np.inf
    t0=time.monotonic()
    val,grad=_lse_score_grad_z(z,beta)
    if not np.isfinite(val): return z,val
    t1=time.monotonic()
    val2,_ = _lse_score_grad_z(z,beta)
    t2=time.monotonic()
    tf=max((t2-t0)/2.0, 1e-5)
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem<2: return z,val
    maxfun=int(max(50, min(maxfun_cap, rem*0.75/tf)))
    if maxfun < 80: return z,val
    bounds=[(-45.0,45.0)] for each? Can't use generator? That's not lambda. `bounds = [(-45.0, 45.0) for _ in range(z.size)]` list comp allowed. OK.
    opts={'maxiter':int(maxfun),'maxfun':int(maxfun),'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-11,'maxls':30,'m':30}
    try:
       res=minimize(_lse_score_grad_z, z, jac=True, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=opts)
    except Exception:
       return z,val
    zout=res.x if hasattr(res,'x') else z
    zout=np.asarray(zout,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    zout=np.nan_to_num(zout,nan=0.0,posinf=45.0,neginf=-45.0)
    zout=np.clip(zout,-45.0,45.0)
    valout,_=_lse_score_grad_z(zout,beta)
    return zout, float(valout)
```
Potential issue: `_lse_score_grad_z` returns (score, grad). When `jac=True`, SciPy may evaluate fun and expect tuple. Good. It may call fun with `args` beta. OK.
`bounds` memory for 10k list of tuples ~ small. Could use sequence? OK.

`_adam_z`:
```
def _adam_z(z0,beta,deadline,max_step=0.25,lr=0.05,beta1=0.9,beta2=0.999,exact_every=150):
    z=np.asarray(z0,dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if z.size==0: return z,np.inf
    m=np.zeros_like(z); v=np.zeros_like(z); t=0
    best_exact_z=z.copy(); best_exact=_exact_fast_z(z)
    best_smooth_val=np.inf; best_smooth_z=z.copy()
    no_improve=0
    lr_cur=float(lr)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.5:
       score, grad = _lse_score_grad_z(z,beta)
       if not np.isfinite(score): break
       if score < best_smooth_val - 1e-12:
          best_smooth_val=score; best_smooth_z=z.copy(); no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve += 1
          if no_improve > 400 and lr_cur > 1e-8:
             lr_cur *= 0.5; no_improve=0
       t += 1
       mb=beta1*m+(1-beta1)*grad
       vb=beta2*v+(1-beta2)*(grad*grad)
       step=lr_cur * mb/(np.sqrt(vb)+1e-8)
       sstep=float(np.linalg.norm(step))
       if sstep > max_step:
          step *= (max_step/sstep)
       z = np.clip(z - step, -45.0, 45.0)
       if t % max(1,int(exact_every)) == 0:
          scz=_exact_fast_z(z)
          if scz < best_exact:
             best_exact=scz; best_exact_z=z.copy()
    scz=_exact_fast_z(z)
    if scz < best_exact:
       best_exact=scz; best_exact_z=z.copy()
    scs=_exact_fast_z(best_smooth_z)
    if scs < best_exact:
       best_exact=scs; best_exact_z=best_smooth_z.copy()
    return best_exact_z, float(best_exact)
```
Potential performance: `exact_every=150` for 10k => many exact (one FFT pair? self only). OK. If loop 60k, exact 400. Good.

`_optimize_start`:
Need call `_lbfgs_z` and `_adam_z`. At each beta, after L-BFGS update, after Adam update. If Adam returns best_exact_z maybe different; we use it as current z? To preserve chain, maybe set current z to `z_adam_out` but it's best exact intermediate. OK.
```
def _optimize_start(a0, deadline, tag=""):
    a=_normalize(a0)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=_exact_fast(a)
    n=a.size
    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 8:
       return best_sc,best_a
    z=_logseq(a)
    if n <= 512:
       betas=[2,10,50,200,1000,5000,20000,100000]
       weights=[1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8]
       maxfun_cap=40000
       adam_lr=0.12; max_step=0.5; exact_every=50
    elif n <= 1024:
       betas=[2,10,50,200,1000,5000,20000,100000]
       weights=[1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8]
       maxfun_cap=20000; lr=0.1; max_step=0.4; exact_every=75
    elif n <= 2048:
       betas=[2,10,50,200,1000,5000,20000]
       weights=[1,2,3,4,5,6,7]
       maxfun_cap=10000; lr=0.08; max_step=0.35; exact_every=100
    elif n <= 4096:
       betas=[2,10,50,200,1000,5000]
       weights=[1,2,3,4,5,6]
       maxfun_cap=6000; lr=0.07; max_step=0.3; exact_every=120
    elif n <= 8192:
       betas=[2,10,50,200,1000,5000,20000]
       weights=[1,2,3,4,5,6,7]
       maxfun_cap=4000; lr=0.06; max_step=0.25; exact_every=150
    else:
       betas=[2,10,50,200,1000,5000]
       weights=[1,2,3,4,5,6]
       maxfun_cap=3000; lr=0.05; max_step=0.25; exact_every=200
    # If beta schedule phase total available
    smooth_dead = deadline - max(5.0, 0.05*(deadline-time.monotonic()))
    total = max(1.0, smooth_dead - time.monotonic())
    sumw=float(sum(weights))
    print progress? Use print only tag and final.
    for idx in range(len(betas)):
       beta=float(betas[idx])
       stage_time=total*float(weights[idx])/sumw
       if stage_time < 6.0:
          continue
       sub_dead=time.monotonic()+stage_time
       lb_dead=time.monotonic()+stage_time*0.55
       if time.monotonic() < lb_dead - 1.0:
          z, _ = _lbfgs_z(z,beta,lb_dead,maxfun_cap)
          a=_softmax(z); sc=_exact_fast(a)
          if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
       adam_dead=sub_dead - 1.0
       if time.monotonic() < adam_dead - 2.0:
          z, sc2 = _adam_z(z,beta,adam_dead,max_step=max_step,lr=adam_lr,exact_every=exact_every)
          if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2<best_sc:
             best_sc=sc2; best_a=_softmax(z).copy()
          # Use current z from adam (best exact) for next
          # Note z may be best exact not smooth; ok
    # final high beta polish
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem > 8.0:
       beta_pol = 20000.0 if n <= 4096 else 10000.0
       z=_logseq(best_a)
       z, scp = _adam_z(z,beta_pol,deadline,max_step=min(0.2,max_step),lr=0.5*adam_lr,exact_every=100)
       if np.isfinite(scp) and scp<best_sc:
          best_sc=scp; best_a=_softmax(z).copy()
    return float(best_sc), best_a
```
Potential bug: If `_lbfgs_z` fails and returns z with val nonfinite, but z may be old. OK.
Stage time uses total computed once; if L-BFGS/Adam fast, sub_dead may pass, next stage still stage_time from original total causing sum <= total if no overrun. But due time, loop can overrun if L-BFGS overshoots lb_dead? `_lbfgs_z` uses maxfun estimated but may overshoot slight. We can check final time in loop. ```

**_pair_polish** implementation with no lambdas.
Need random generator. Top-level function.
```
def _pair_polish(a0, deadline, rng, guide_beta=2000):
    a=_normalize(a0); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=_exact_fast(a); n=a.size
    if n<2 or time.monotonic() > deadline-3: return best_sc,best_a
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 3:
        # Compute guidance
        score, grad_a = _lse_grad_a(a, guide_beta)
        if not np.isfinite(score): break
        R = grad_a / (4.0*float(n)) if n>0
        Qc = float(a.dot(R))
        g = R - Qc
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0)
        # top/bottom pools
        kpool = min(300, n)
        if n == 1: break
        order = np.argsort(g)
        low_pool = order[:kpool]
        high_pool = order[-kpool:]
        # ensure a positive in pools
        high_mask = a[high_pool] > 0.0
        if not np.any(high_mask):
            # fallback high by a
            high_pool = np.argsort(a)[-kpool:]
        low_mask = a[low_pool] > 0.0
        if not np.any(low_mask):
            low_pool = np.argsort(a)[:kpool]
        high_pool = high_pool[high_mask]
        low_pool = low_pool[low_mask]
        if high_pool.size==0 or low_pool.size==0: break
        # active peak sources
        c=_self_conv_fft(a); d=n*c; dmax=float(d.max())
        # top peaks
        if d.size>0:
           top_peaks=np.argsort(d)[-min(5,d.size):]
        else: top_peaks=np.array([])
        # batch for 2 seconds
        batch_dead = min(deadline, time.monotonic()+2.0)
        accepted=0
        while time.monotonic() < batch_dead:
            # choose source: 70% from high_pool, 30% active
            if len(top_peaks)>0 and rng.random()<0.3:
                k=int(top_peaks[int(rng.integers(len(top_peaks)))])
                lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
                if hi<lo: source=i? continue
                # choose i from valid range weighted by a_i*a_{k-i}
                idxs=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
                contrib=a[idxs]*a[k-idxs]
                totalc=contrib.sum()
                if totalc<=0:
                    source=int(idxs[0])
                else:
                    p=contrib/totalc
                    source=int(idxs[int(rng.choice(len(idxs), p=p))])
            else:
                source=int(high_pool[int(rng.integers(high_pool.size))])
            dest=int(low_pool[int(rng.integers(low_pool.size))])
            if source==dest: continue
            if a[source]<=0: continue
            # choose fraction
            frac = float(rng.choice([0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4]))
            delta=frac*a[source]
            cand=a.copy(); cand[source]-=delta; cand[dest]+=delta
            if cand[source]<-1e-15: continue
            cand=np.clip(cand,0.0,None); sc=_exact_fast(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_sc:
                best_sc=sc; best_a=cand.copy(); a=cand.copy(); accepted+=1
                # occasionally recompute guidance if multiple improvements
                if accepted>=8: break
            # Very small acceptance? We break only if accepted>=8 to refresh
            # To avoid long no improvement? The batch will end in 2s and recompute.
        if accepted>=8:
            continue
    return best_sc,best_a
```
Need `rng.choice` with p for source: `rng.choice(len(idxs), p=p)` (NumPy Generator.choice can accept array index or int? If int n, p array, returns int). OK.
But using `cand=a.copy()` per trial 10k, and exact fast, maybe 2s batch maybe 1000 trials, okay. Memory.
`guide_beta=2000` for n large; weights maybe not very sharp. Use maybe 10000 for n=10k? `guide_beta=10000` if n>4096 else 2000. Implement:
```
if n>8192: guide_beta=10000 else 5000
```
But _lse_grad_a at beta=10000 computes w, R. Fine.

**_perturb_a**:
```
def _perturb_a(a, rng, strength=None):
   a=_normalize(a); n=a.size
   if strength is None: strength=float(rng.uniform(0.15,0.5))
   mode=int(rng.integers(0,6))
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   if mode==0:
      q=a*np.exp(0.5*strength*rng.standard_normal(n))
   elif mode==1:
      t=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.3)); q=(1-t)*a + t*np.ones(n)/n
   elif mode==2:
      scale=float(rng.uniform(0.005,0.1)); alpha=float(strength*10)
      edge=np.exp(-x/scale)+np.exp(-(1-x)/scale); q=a*(1+alpha*edge/edge.max())
   elif mode==3:
      # smooth noise
      N=_next_pow2(n); freqs=np.fft.rfftfreq(N); power=1/((n*np.abs(freqs)+1)**2); power[0]=0; phase=rng.normal(size=len(power)); noise=np.fft.irfft(power*np.exp(1j*phase),N)[:n]; noise-=noise.mean(); q=a*np.exp(0.3*strength*noise)
   elif mode==4:
      # neighbor swap/mix
      q=a.copy(); ns=int(max(20,n//100)); for loop? Use vectorized shifts: for s in rng int?
      s=int(rng.integers(1,max(2,n//100))); q=0.5*a+0.5*np.roll(a,s)
   else:
      q=a*np.exp(0.2*strength*rng.standard_normal(n)) + 0.01
   q=_normalize(np.clip(q,0.0,None))
   return q
```
No nested loops? For mode 4, `s` computed. OK.
Could use `np.roll`, length. Good.

**_select_starts** no lambda:
```
def _rank_starts(starts, k=3):
    scored=[]
    for arr in starts:
       arr=_normalize(np.asarray(arr,dtype=float).ravel())
       if arr.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): continue
       sc=_exact_fast(arr)
       if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc, arr))
    if not scored: return []
    scores=[s for s,_ in scored]
    order=np.argsort(np.asarray(scores))
    top=[]
    for idx in order[:k]:
       top.append(scored[int(idx)])
    return top
```

Let's write final code fully. Need be mindful `rng` calls, no lambdas.

**Time management detail in propose**:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    budget=float(budget_s)
    deadline=t_start+budget-90.0
    if deadline < t_start+60: deadline=t_start+60
    rng=np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    parent=None
    try:
       h=globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
       if h is not None:
          p=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
          p=np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
          p=np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0)
          if p.size>=64 and p.sum()>0:
             parent=p
    except Exception:
       parent=None
    # target length
    if parent is not None:
       target_len=parent.size
       if target_len < 2048: target_len=4096
       if target_len > 16384: target_len=16384
    else:
       target_len=10240
    # global best
    if parent is not None:
       best_a=_normalize(_resample(parent,target_len)) if parent.size != target_len else _normalize(parent)
    else:
       best_a=np.ones(target_len)/target_len
    best_sc=_exact_fast(best_a)
    print("initial best {:.8f} len {} parent {}".format(best_sc,best_a.size,"yes" if parent is not None else "no"))
    # multi-res
    levels=[]
    L=512
    max_pre_len = min(8192, target_len)
    while L < max_pre_len:
       levels.append(L); L*=2
    # if target_len > 8192 include 8192 already
    if 8192 in levels and target_len > 8192: # ok
       pass
    # pre time
    if parent is not None:
       pre_total = min(480.0, max(120.0, budget*0.22))
    else:
       pre_total = min(850.0, max(300.0, budget*0.35))
    pre_dead = t_start + pre_total
    if time.monotonic() < pre_dead - 20 and len(levels)>0:
       weights_pre=[1,2,4,8,16][:len(levels)]
       sumw=float(sum(weights_pre))
       for idx,L in enumerate(levels):
          if time.monotonic() > pre_dead - 20: break
          stage_dead = t_start + pre_total * float(sum(weights_pre[:idx+1]))/sumw
          stage_dead = min(stage_dead, pre_dead - 10)
          if time.monotonic() > stage_dead - 5: continue
          inits=[]
          if parent is not None:
             inits.append(_normalize(_resample(parent,L)))
          # add best_a resampled
          inits.append(_normalize(_resample(best_a,L)))
          inits.extend(_structured_inits(L, rng, parent, cap=25))
          top=_rank_starts(inits, k=3)
          if not top: continue
          # optimize top 1 for 65% of stage, top2 for 35%
          stage_time = stage_dead - time.monotonic()
          # first
          sc, arr2 = _optimize_start(top[0][1], time.monotonic() + stage_time*0.65, tag="preL{}".format(L))
          if sc < best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best_a=arr2.copy()
          if len(top)>=2 and time.monotonic() < stage_dead - 10:
             # start second maybe blend with current best? resample best to L
             blend_start=_normalize(_resample(best_a,L))
             # Could also top[1]
             sc2, arr3 = _optimize_start(top[1][1], stage_dead - 2, tag="preL{}_b".format(L))
             if sc2 < best_sc:
                best_sc=sc2; best_a=arr3.copy()
          print("pre level {} best {:.8f}".format(L,best_sc))
    # final stage
    final_dead = deadline - 80.0  # leave for final polish and verify
    final_budget = max(60.0, final_dead - time.monotonic())
    final_inits=[]
    if parent is not None:
       final_inits.append(_normalize(_resample(parent,target_len)))
    final_inits.append(_normalize(_resample(best_a,target_len)))
    final_inits.append(_normalize(best_a[::-1]))
    # generate final structured inits top maybe
    final_inits.extend(_structured_inits(target_len, rng, parent, cap=20))
    top_final=_rank_starts(final_inits, k=6)
    if not top_final:
       top_final=[(best_sc,_normalize(best_a))]
    # choose number starts based final_budget
    if final_budget > 1500:
       n_starts=3; fracs=[0.45,0.30,0.25]
    elif final_budget > 800:
       n_starts=2; fracs=[0.55,0.45]
    else:
       n_starts=1; fracs=[1.0]
    # Ensure distinct? top final sorted by score. Use first n_starts.
    cum=0.0
    for r in range(min(n_starts, len(top_final))):
       if time.monotonic() > final_dead - 40: break
       frac=fracs[r]
       # For subsequent starts, blend with best to improve? maybe if r>0:
       start_arr=top_final[r][1]
       if r>0:
          best_res=_normalize(_resample(best_a,target_len))
          start_arr=_normalize(0.5*start_arr+0.5*best_res)
       sub_dead=time.monotonic() + final_budget*frac
       sub_dead=min(sub_dead, final_dead - 20)
       if time.monotonic() <= sub_dead - 10:
          sc, arr2=_optimize_start(start_arr, sub_dead, tag="fin{}".format(r+1))
          if sc<best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best_a=arr2.copy()
             print("final start{} {:.8f}".format(r+1,best_sc))
    # Perturb restarts if time
    if time.monotonic() < final_dead - 80:
       # do up to 3 restarts
       restarts=3
       chunk=(final_dead - 80 - time.monotonic())/restarts
       for ri in range(restarts):
          if chunk < 30: break
          sub_dead=time.monotonic()+chunk
          p_start=_perturb_a(best_a, rng, strength=0.25)
          sc, arr2=_optimize_start(p_start, sub_dead, tag="pert{}".format(ri+1))
          if sc<best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best_a=arr2.copy(); print("perturb{} {:.8f}".format(ri+1,best_sc))
    # Final pair polish using remaining until deadline-40
    polish_dead = min(deadline - 30.0, t_start + budget - 35.0)
    if time.monotonic() < polish_dead - 20:
       sc, arr2=_pair_polish(best_a, polish_dead, rng)
       if sc<best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=arr2.copy()
          print("pair polish {:.8f}".format(best_sc))
    # Ensure output
    out_arr=np.asarray(best_a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out_arr=np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_arr=np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S=float(out_arr.sum())
    if S < 0.01:
       out_arr=np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)*0.001? Need sum >0.01. Use 0.5? sum 512.
    else:
       out_arr=out_arr/S
    # If out_arr length 0 fallback
    if out_arr.size == 0: out_arr=np.ones(1024)
    out=[float(x) for x in out_arr]
    # verify if time and evaluate exists
    verified=np.nan
    try:
       if time.monotonic() < t_start + budget - 10:
          verified=float(evaluate_sequence(out))
          if np.isfinite(verified) and verified < best_sc:
             best_sc=verified
          print("FINAL internal {:.8f} verified {:.8f} len {} time {:.1f}".format(best_sc, verified, len(out), time.monotonic()-t_start))
       else:
          print("FINAL internal {:.8f} len {} time {:.1f}".format(best_sc, len(out), time.monotonic()-t_start))
    except Exception as e:
       print("FINAL internal {:.8f} len {} time {:.1f} verify_error {}".format(best_sc,len(out),time.monotonic()-t_start,e))
    return out
```
Need fallback if S<0.01: `out_arr=np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)` sum1024. Good.

Concerns:
- `_structured_inits` returns list of arrays; each normalized maybe norm. Good.
- `_pair_polish` uses `_lse_grad_a` which returns smooth score and grad. If `grad_a` all zeros (shouldn't), R zeros. OK.
- In `_pair_polish`, `c=_self_conv_fft(a); d=n*c; top_peaks=np.argsort(d)[-min(5,d.size):]` Note if min returns int, `np.argsort(d)[-0:]`? If d.size>0, min(5,d.size)>=1. OK.
- `rng.choice(len(idxs), p=p)`: if len(idxs)==1, OK. If p contains zeros? yes. It returns np.int64, int.
- In `_structured_inits`, `rng.normal(size=len(power))` works? `rng.normal` default loc=0 scale=1; size keyword OK. Or use `rng.standard_normal(len(power))`. Good.

**Potential issue with `minimize` and global function**: `minimize(_lse_score_grad_z, z, jac=True, args=(beta,), ...)`. Since `_lse_score_grad_z` returns tuple, good. But when `jac=True`, SciPy expects fun to return (f,g). It also may check `jac` is True; OK.

**Potential issue with `bounds` list**: For n=10240, list comp OK but uses Python loop; fine.

**Potential issue: `_lbfgs_z` uses `val2,_` which may be expensive. Could skip estimate by fixed maxfun based on time? But OK.

**Potential issue: `tf` may be too low due CPU warmup; maxfun huge causing L-BFGS overshoot. Cap limits. For n=10240 cap 3000, 3000*0.005=15s; stage 100*0.55=55s, so cap dominates. It may underuse. But cap 3000 maybe enough? Maybe need more iterations. Could set cap higher for final large: 6000. Time 6000*0.005=30s. OK. For 10240 maybe 6000. **Let's write `_structured_inits` more concise but many appends. Need ensure no helper nested. We can use `if len(inits) < cap:` repeated maybe.

```
def _structured_inits(n, rng, parent, cap=30):
    inits=[]
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    if parent is not None:
       pr=_normalize(_resample(parent,n)); inits.append(pr); inits.append(pr[::-1])
       edge=np.exp(-x/0.02)+np.exp(-(1-x)/0.02)
       if edge.max()>0:
          for alpha in [1.0,5.0,20.0]:
             inits.append(_normalize(pr*(1.0+alpha*edge/edge.max())))
    inits.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    for p in ...:
       ...
       inits.append(...)
    # after all, if len > cap: keep variety by selecting evenly? Use first cap? Better use top quick? But before rank we cap. We can cap by taking first half + random? Simpler: if len(inits)>cap: step=len(inits)//cap; inits=[inits[i] for i in range(0,len(inits),max(1,step))]; if less fill last.
    # ensure all finite; filter
    out=[]
    for arr in inits:
       arr=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if arr.size==n and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
          arr=_normalize(arr)
          if float(arr.sum())>0:
             out.append(arr)
       if len(out)>=cap: break
    return out
```
Cap by first order. Since first includes parent, uniform, p shapes. Good.

Add:
```
# sqrt-flat init
Ld=_next_pow2(2*n)
Btarget=np.zeros(Ld); first=2*n-1; Btarget[:first]=1.0
Bf=np.fft.rfft(Btarget, Ld)
Af=np.sqrt(Bf)  # complex
Ainit=np.fft.irfft(Af, Ld)
# try variants: clip, abs, positive part of Ainit[:n], Ainit[n-1:2n-1]? Also resample full to n
```

Define `_to_len` top-level.
```
def _to_len(a,n):
   if n<=0: return np.empty(0)
   arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
   if arr.size != n:
      arr=_resample(arr,n)
   return _normalize(arr)
```

**Detailed `_structured_inits` code**:
```
def _structured_inits(n, rng, parent, cap=30):
    inits = []
    if n <= 0:
        return inits
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    if parent is not None:
        pr = _to_len(parent, n)
        inits.append(pr)
        inits.append(pr[::-1])
        edge = np.exp(-x / 0.02) + np.exp(-(1.0 - x) / 0.02)
        emax = float(edge.max())
        if emax > 0.0:
            for alpha in (1.0, 5.0, 20.0):
                inits.append(_normalize(pr * (1.0 + alpha * edge / emax)))
    inits.append(np.ones(n) / float(n))
    # sqrt-flat
    g = _sqrt_flat_coeffs(n)
    u = 0.5 * (g + g[::-1])
    inits.append(_normalize(g))
    inits.append(_normalize(g[::-1]))
    inits.append(_normalize(u))
    for alpha in (0.5, 2.0):
        gp = np.power(g, alpha)
        inits.append(_normalize(gp))
        up = 0.5 * (gp + gp[::-1])
        inits.append(_normalize(up))
    # power U
    xx = np.linspace(1e-6, 1.0 - 1e-6, n)
    for p in (0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.45, 0.6, 0.8):
        arr = np.power(xx, -p) * np.power(1.0 - xx, -p)
        arr = arr + 1e-5
        inits.append(_normalize(np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1e9)))
    # V shapes
    for alpha in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0):
        inits.append(_normalize(1.0 + alpha * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2))
        inits.append(_normalize(1.0 + alpha * np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0)))
        inits.append(_normalize(1.0 + alpha * (1.0 - np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0))))
    # edge exponentials
    for scale in (0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1):
        edge = np.exp(-x / max(scale, 1e-6)) + np.exp(-(1.0 - x) / max(scale, 1e-6))
        emax = float(edge.max())
        if emax > 0:
            for alpha in (1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0):
                inits.append(_normalize(1.0 + alpha * edge / emax))
    # cos
    for f in (1, 2, 3, 4):
        for amp in (0.5, 1.5, 3.0):
            inits.append(_normalize(np.clip(1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x), 0.0, None)))
            inits.append(_normalize(np.clip(1.0 + amp * np.cos(np.pi * f * x), 0.0, None)))
    # spectrum sqrt flat
    try:
        Ld = _next_pow2(2 * n)
        btarget = np.zeros(Ld, dtype=np.float64)
        btarget[:2 * n - 1] = 1.0
        bfft = np.fft.fft(btarget)
        ainit = np.fft.ifft(np.sqrt(bfft)).real
        if ainit.size >= n:
            mid = ainit.size - n
            inits.append(_normalize(np.abs(ainit[:n])))
            inits.append(_normalize(np.clip(ainit[:n], 0.0, None)))
            inits.append(_normalize(np.abs(ainit[mid:mid+n])))
        inits.append(_normalize(_to_len(np.abs(ainit), n)))
    except Exception:
        pass
    # random smooth
    Nf = _next_pow2(n)
    for _ in range(5):
        freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf)
        power = 1.0 / (n * np.abs(freqs) + 1.0) ** float(rng.uniform(1.0, 3.0))
        power[0] = 0.0
        phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=len(power))
        noise = np.fft.irfft(power * np.exp(1j * phase), Nf)[:n]
        noise -= noise.mean()
        inits.append(_normalize(np.clip(1.0 + float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.0)) * noise, 0.0, None)))
    # random
    for _ in range(3):
        inits.append(_normalize(0.01 + 0.99 * rng.random(n)))
    # cap/filter
    out = []
    for arr in inits:
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size == n:
            arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
            if float(arr.sum()) > 1e-15:
                out.append(_normalize(arr))
        if len(out) >= cap:
            break
    return out
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

The implementation uses:
- multi-resolution warm starts (low detail to full length),
- structured initializers inspired by the formal square-root of a flat
  auto-convolution, endpoint-sparse U-shapes, and spectral heuristics,
- softmax parameterization with LSE-smoothed max-convolution objectives,
- L-BFGS-B for smooth phases plus Adam polishing in log-simplex space,
- final exact-guided pairwise mass transfers for local peak reduction.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_ZMIN = -45.0
_ZMAX = 45.0


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def _self_conv_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:M]
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return out


def _exact_fast(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    S = float(arr.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    c = _self_conv_fft(arr)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    val = 2.0 * float(n) * float(c.max()) / (S * S)
    return float(val) if np.isfinite(val) else float("inf")


def _resample(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    m = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(a.sum())], dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a).astype(np.float64)


def _to_len(a, n):
    return _normalize(_resample(a, n))


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=_ZMAX, neginf=_ZMIN)
    z = np.clip(z, _ZMIN, _ZMAX)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(z.size)
    return e / s


def _logseq(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, np.exp(_ZMIN), None)
    return np.log(a)


def _sqrt_flat_coeffs(n):
    g = np.ones(max(1, int(n)), dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 1:
        k = np.arange(1, int(n), dtype=np.float64)
        g[1:] = np.cumprod(1.0 - 0.5 / k)
    return g


def _lse_grad_a(a, beta):
    """Smooth LSE objective and its gradient w.r.t. normalized a."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-15:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / S

    beta = float(beta)
    if not np.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 0.0:
        beta = 1.0

    Nself = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    Ar = np.fft.rfft(a, Nself)
    c = np.fft.irfft(Ar * Ar, Nself)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    d = float(n) * c
    dmax = float(d.max())
    if not np.isfinite(dmax):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    s = beta * (d - dmax)
    s = np.clip(s, -400.0, 400.0)
    e = np.exp(s)
    sume = float(e.sum())
    if sume <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sume):
        w = np.full(2 * n - 1, 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
        L = dmax
    else:
        w = e / sume
        L = dmax + float(np.log(sume)) / beta

    w_rev = w[::-1]
    Ncross = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    if Ncross == Nself:
        Across = Ar
    else:
        Across = np.fft.rfft(a, Ncross)
    WR = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, Ncross)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(Across * WR, Ncross)
    R = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    R = np.nan_to_num(R, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    score = 2.0 * float(L)
    grad_a = 4.0 * float(n) * R
    return float(score), grad_a


def _lse_score_grad_z(z, beta):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = _softmax(z)
    score, grad_a = _lse_grad_a(a, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(z, dtype=np.float64)
    mu = float(a.dot(grad_a))
    grad_z = grad_a - a * mu
    grad_z = np.nan_to_num(grad_z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return float(score), grad_z


def _exact_fast_z(z):
    return _exact_fast(_softmax(z))


def _lbfgs_z(z0, beta, deadline, maxfun_cap=3000):
    z = np.asarray(z0, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if z.size == 0:
        return z, float("inf")

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    val, _ = _lse_score_grad_z(z, beta)
    tf = max(time.monotonic() - t0, 1e-5)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return z, val

    rem = max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.monotonic())
    if rem < 2.0:
        return z, val

    maxfun = int(max(80, min(int(maxfun_cap), rem * 0.75 / tf)))
    if maxfun < 80:
        return z, val

    bounds = [(_ZMIN, _ZMAX)] * int(z.size)
    opts = {
        "maxiter": maxfun,
        "maxfun": maxfun,
        "ftol": 1e-14,
        "gtol": 1e-11,
        "maxls": 30,
        "m": 30,
    }
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _lse_score_grad_z,
            z,
            jac=True,
            args=(beta,),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=bounds,
            options=opts,
        )
    except Exception:
        return z, float(val)

    zout = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel() if hasattr(res, "x") else z
    zout = np.nan_to_num(zout, nan=0.0, posinf=_ZMAX, neginf=_ZMIN)
    zout = np.clip(zout, _ZMIN, _ZMAX)
    valout, _ = _lse_score_grad_z(zout, beta)
    return zout, float(valout)


def _adam_z(z0, beta, deadline, max_step=0.25, lr=0.05, beta1=0.9, beta2=0.999, exact_every=150):
    z = np.asarray(z0, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=_ZMAX, neginf=_ZMIN)
    z = np.clip(z, _ZMIN, _ZMAX)
    if z.size == 0:
        return z, float("inf")

    m = np.zeros_like(z, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros_like(z, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    best_exact_z = z.copy()
    best_exact = _exact_fast_z(z)
    best_smooth_val = float("inf")
    best_smooth_z = z.copy()
    no_improve = 0
    lr_cur = float(lr)
    exact_every = max(10, int(exact_every))

    while time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 0.5:
        score, grad = _lse_score_grad_z(z, beta)
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            break

        if score < best_smooth_val - 1e-12:
            best_smooth_val = float(score)
            best_smooth_z = z.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 400 and lr_cur > 1e-8:
                lr_cur *= 0.5
                no_improve = 0

        t += 1
        m = beta1 * m + (1.0 - beta1) * grad
        v = beta2 * v + (1.0 - beta2) * (grad * grad)
        step = lr_cur * m / (np.sqrt(v) + 1e-8)
        sstep = float(np.linalg.norm(step))
        if sstep > max_step:
            step *= (max_step / sstep)
        z = np.clip(z - step, _ZMIN, _ZMAX)

        if t % exact_every == 0:
            scz = _exact_fast_z(z)
            if scz < best_exact:
                best_exact = scz
                best_exact_z = z.copy()

    scz = _exact_fast_z(z)
    if scz < best_exact:
        best_exact = scz
        best_exact_z = z.copy()

    scs = _exact_fast_z(best_smooth_z)
    if scs < best_exact:
        best_exact = scs
        best_exact_z = best_smooth_z.copy()

    return best_exact_z, float(best_exact)


def _optimize_start(a0, deadline, tag=""):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _exact_fast(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 8.0:
        return float(best_sc), best_a

    z = _logseq(a)

    if n <= 512:
        betas = [1, 5, 20, 100, 500, 2500, 10000, 50000]
        weights = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8]
        maxfun_cap = 40000
        adam_lr = 0.12
        max_step = 0.5
        exact_every = 50
    elif n <= 1024:
        betas = [1, 5, 20, 100, 500, 2500, 10000, 50000]
        weights = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8]
        maxfun_cap = 20000
        adam_lr = 0.10
        max_step = 0.4
        exact_every = 75
    elif n <= 2048:
        betas = [1, 5, 20, 100, 500, 2500, 10000]
        weights = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7]
        maxfun_cap = 10000
        adam_lr = 0.08
        max_step = 0.35
        exact_every = 100
    elif n <= 4096:
        betas = [1, 5, 20, 100, 500, 2500, 10000]
        weights = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7]
        maxfun_cap = 6000
        adam_lr = 0.07
        max_step = 0.3
        exact_every = 120
    elif n <= 8192:
        betas = [1, 5, 20, 100, 500, 2500, 10000]
        weights = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7]
        maxfun_cap = 4000
        adam_lr = 0.06
        max_step = 0.25
        exact_every = 150
    else:
        betas = [1, 5, 20, 100, 500, 2500, 10000]
        weights = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7]
        maxfun_cap = 3000
        adam_lr = 0.05
        max_step = 0.25
        exact_every = 200

    smooth_reserve = max(5.0, 0.05 * max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.monotonic()))
    smooth_dead = float(deadline) - smooth_reserve
    total = max(1.0, smooth_dead - time.monotonic())
    sumw = float(sum(weights))

    for idx in range(len(betas)):
        if time.monotonic() > smooth_dead - 5.0:
            break
        beta = float(betas[idx])
        stage_time = total * float(weights[idx]) / sumw
        if stage_time < 6.0:
            continue
        sub_dead = time.monotonic() + stage_time
        sub_dead = min(sub_dead, smooth_dead)

        lb_dead = time.monotonic() + 0.55 * stage_time
        lb_dead = min(lb_dead, sub_dead)
        if time.monotonic() < lb_dead - 1.0:
            z, _ = _lbfgs_z(z, beta, lb_dead, maxfun_cap)
            a = _softmax(z)
            sc = _exact_fast(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a.copy()

        adam_dead = sub_dead - 1.0
        if time.monotonic() < adam_dead - 2.0:
            z, sc2 = _adam_z(z, beta, adam_dead, max_step=max_step, lr=adam_lr, exact_every=exact_every)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc2
                best_a = _softmax(z).copy()

    rem = float(deadline) - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 8.0:
        beta_pol = 20000.0 if n <= 4096 else 10000.0
        zpol = _logseq(best_a)
        zpol, scp = _adam_z(
            zpol,
            beta_pol,
            deadline,
            max_step=min(0.2, max_step),
            lr=0.5 * adam_lr,
            exact_every=100,
        )
        if np.isfinite(scp) and scp < best_sc:
            best_sc = scp
            best_a = _softmax(zpol).copy()

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def _structured_inits(n, rng, parent, cap=30):
    inits = []
    if n <= 0:
        return inits

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    if parent is not None:
        pr = _to_len(parent, n)
        inits.append(pr)
        inits.append(pr[::-1])
        edge = np.exp(-x / 0.02) + np.exp(-(1.0 - x) / 0.02)
        emax = float(edge.max())
        if emax > 0.0:
            for alpha in (1.0, 5.0, 20.0):
                inits.append(_normalize(pr * (1.0 + alpha * edge / emax)))

    inits.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

    g = _sqrt_flat_coeffs(n)
    u = 0.5 * (g + g[::-1])
    inits.append(_normalize(g))
    inits.append(_normalize(g[::-1]))
    inits.append(_normalize(u))

    for alpha in (0.5, 2.0):
        gp = np.power(g, alpha)
        inits.append(_normalize(gp))
        up = 0.5 * (gp + gp[::-1])
        inits.append(_normalize(up))

    xx = np.linspace(1e-6, 1.0 - 1e-6, n)
    for p in (0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.45, 0.6, 0.8):
        arr = np.power(xx, -p) * np.power(1.0 - xx, -p) + 1e-5
        inits.append(_normalize(np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1e9)))

    for alpha in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0):
        inits.append(_normalize(1.0 + alpha * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2))
        inits.append(_normalize(1.0 + alpha * np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0)))
        inits.append(_normalize(1.0 + alpha * (1.0 - np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0))))

    for scale in (0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1):
        edge = np.exp(-x / max(scale, 1e-6)) + np.exp(-(1.0 - x) / max(scale, 1e-6))
        emax = float(edge.max())
        if emax > 0.0:
            for alpha in (1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0):
                inits.append(_normalize(1.0 + alpha * edge / emax))

    for f in (1, 2, 3, 4):
        for amp in (0.5, 1.5, 3.0):
            inits.append(_normalize(np.clip(1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(f) * x), 0.0, None)))
            inits.append(_normalize(np.clip(1.0 + amp * np.cos(np.pi * float(f) * x), 0.0, None)))

    try:
        Ld = _next_pow2(2 * n)
        btarget = np.zeros(Ld, dtype=np.float64)
        btarget[:2 * n - 1] = 1.0
        bfft = np.fft.fft(btarget)
        ainit = np.fft.ifft(np.sqrt(bfft)).real
        if ainit.size >= n:
            mid = max(0, ainit.size - n)
            inits.append(_normalize(np.abs(ainit[:n])))
            inits.append(_normalize(np.clip(ainit[:n], 0.0, None)))
            inits.append(_normalize(np.abs(ainit[mid:mid + n])))
        inits.append(_normalize(_to_len(np.abs(ainit), n)))
    except Exception:
        pass

    Nf = _next_pow2(n)
    for _ in range(5):
        freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf)
        power = 1.0 / (float(n) * np.abs(freqs) + 1.0) ** float(rng.uniform(1.0, 3.0))
        power[0] = 0.0
        phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=len(power))
        noise = np.fft.irfft(power * np.exp(1j * phase), Nf)[:n]
        noise -= float(noise.mean())
        inits.append(_normalize(np.clip(1.0 + float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.0)) * noise, 0.0, None)))

    for _ in range(3):
        inits.append(_normalize(0.01 + 0.99 * rng.random(n)))

    out = []
    for arr in inits:
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size == n:
            arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
            if float(arr.sum()) > 1e-15:
                out.append(_normalize(arr))
        if len(out) >= int(cap):
            break
    return out


def _rank_starts(starts, k=3):
    scored = []
    for arr in starts:
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size == 0:
            continue
        arr = _normalize(arr)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            continue
        sc = _exact_fast(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, arr))
    if not scored:
        return []
    scores = np.array([s for s, _ in scored], dtype=np.float64)
    order = np.argsort(scores)
    top = []
    for idx in order[:max(1, int(k))]:
        top.append(scored[int(idx)])
    return top


def _perturb_a(a, rng, strength=None):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if strength is None:
        strength = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.45))
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 6))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    if mode == 0:
        q = a * np.exp(0.5 * strength * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 1:
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.3))
        q = (1.0 - t) * a + t * (np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
    elif mode == 2:
        scale = float(rng.uniform(0.005, 0.1))
        alpha = float(strength * 10.0)
        edge = np.exp(-x / scale) + np.exp(-(1.0 - x) / scale)
        emax = max(1e-15, float(edge.max()))
        q = a * (1.0 + alpha * edge / emax)
    elif mode == 3:
        Nf = _next_pow2(n)
        freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf)
        power = 1.0 / (float(n) * np.abs(freqs) + 1.0) ** 2.0
        power[0] = 0.0
        phase = rng.standard_normal(len(power))
        noise = np.fft.irfft(power * np.exp(1j * phase), Nf)[:n]
        noise -= float(noise.mean())
        q = a * np.exp(0.3 * strength * noise)
    elif mode == 4:
        s = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 100)))
        q = 0.5 * a + 0.5 * np.roll(a, s)
    else:
        q = a * np.exp(0.2 * strength * rng.standard_normal(n)) + 0.01

    return _normalize(np.clip(q, 0.0, None))


def _pair_polish(a0, deadline, rng, guide_beta=5000):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _exact_fast(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 3.0:
        return float(best_sc), best_a

    gb = float(guide_beta)
    if n > 8192:
        gb = 10000.0
    elif n > 2048:
        gb = 5000.0
    else:
        gb = 2000.0

    while time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 2.0:
        score, grad_a = _lse_grad_a(a, gb)
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            break
        R = grad_a / (4.0 * float(n))
        Qc = float(a.dot(R))
        g = R - Qc
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

        kpool = min(300, n)
        order = np.argsort(g)
        low_pool = order[:kpool]
        high_pool = order[-kpool:]

        high_mask = a[high_pool] > 0.0
        if not np.any(high_mask):
            high_pool = np.argsort(a)[-kpool:]
            high_mask = a[high_pool] > 0.0
        low_mask = a[low_pool] > 0.0
        if not np.any(low_mask):
            low_pool = np.argsort(a)[:kpool]
            low_mask = a[low_pool] > 0.0

        high_pool = high_pool[high_mask]
        low_pool = low_pool[low_mask]
        if high_pool.size == 0 or low_pool.size == 0:
            break

        c = _self_conv_fft(a)
        d = float(n) * c
        if d.size > 0:
            top_peaks = np.argsort(d)[-min(5, d.size):]
        else:
            top_peaks = np.array([], dtype=int)

        batch_dead = min(float(deadline), time.monotonic() + 2.0)
        improved = False
        while time.monotonic() < batch_dead:
            if len(top_peaks) > 0 and rng.random() < 0.30:
                k = int(top_peaks[int(rng.integers(len(top_peaks)))])
                lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
                hi = min(n - 1, k)
                if hi < lo:
                    continue
                idxs = np.arange(lo, hi + 1)
                contrib = a[idxs] * a[k - idxs]
                totalc = float(contrib.sum())
                if totalc <= 0.0:
                    source = int(idxs[0])
                else:
                    p = contrib / totalc
                    source = int(idxs[int(rng.choice(len(idxs), p=p))])
            else:
                source = int(high_pool[int(rng.integers(high_pool.size))])

            dest = int(low_pool[int(rng.integers(low_pool.size))])
            if source == dest or a[source] <= 0.0:
                continue

            frac = float(rng.choice([0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40]))
            delta = frac * a[source]
            cand = a.copy()
            cand[source] -= delta
            cand[dest] += delta
            if cand[source] < 0.0:
                continue
            cand = _normalize(cand)
            sc = _exact_fast(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = cand.copy()
                a = cand.copy()
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            continue

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Return a list of non-negative heights minimizing the evaluation function."""
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    hard_dead = t_start + budget - 20.0
    if hard_dead < t_start + 60.0:
        hard_dead = t_start + 60.0
    search_dead = max(t_start + 30.0, hard_dead - 50.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 42)

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 64 and float(p.sum()) > 0.0:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        target_len = int(parent.size)
        if target_len < 2048:
            target_len = 4096
        if target_len > 16384:
            target_len = 16384
    else:
        target_len = 10240

    if parent is not None:
        best_a = _to_len(parent, target_len)
    else:
        best_a = np.ones(target_len, dtype=np.float64) / float(target_len)
    best_sc = _exact_fast(best_a)
    print("initial best: {: .8f}, len={}".format(float(best_sc), int(best_a.size)))

    # Multi-resolution warm-up.
    max_pre_len = min(8192, target_len)
    levels = []
    L = 512
    while L <= max_pre_len:
        levels.append(int(L))
        L *= 2

    if parent is not None:
        pre_total = 480.0
    else:
        pre_total = 850.0

    # Keep enough time for the full-length stage.
    if parent is not None:
        max_pre_allowed = min(600.0, max(0.0, search_dead - t_start - 1200.0))
    else:
        max_pre_allowed = min(950.0, max(0.0, search_dead - t_start - 1200.0))
    pre_total = min(pre_total, max_pre_allowed)
    pre_dead = t_start + pre_total
    pre_dead = min(pre_dead, search_dead - 500.0)

    if len(levels) > 0 and pre_total > 60.0 and time.monotonic() < pre_dead - 20.0:
        weights_pre = [1, 2, 4, 8, 16][:len(levels)]
        while len(weights_pre) < len(levels):
            weights_pre.append(16)
        sumw_pre = float(sum(weights_pre))

        for idx, L in enumerate(levels):
            if time.monotonic() > pre_dead - 20.0:
                break
            cum = sum(weights_pre[:idx + 1])
            stage_dead = t_start + pre_total * float(cum) / sumw_pre
            stage_dead = min(stage_dead, pre_dead - 10.0)
            if time.monotonic() > stage_dead - 5.0:
                continue

            inits = []
            inits.append(_to_len(best_a, L))
            if parent is not None:
                inits.append(_to_len(parent, L))
            inits.extend(_structured_inits(L, rng, parent, cap=25))

            top = _rank_starts(inits, k=3)
            if not top:
                continue

            stage_time = max(0.0, stage_dead - time.monotonic())
            if stage_time > 10.0:
                sc, arr2 = _optimize_start(top[0][1], time.monotonic() + 0.65 * stage_time, tag="preL{}".format(L))
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_a = arr2.copy()

            if len(top) >= 2 and time.monotonic() < stage_dead - 10.0:
                sc2, arr3 = _optimize_start(top[1][1], stage_dead - 2.0, tag="preL{}_b".format(L))
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_a = arr3.copy()

            print("pre level {}: best={:.8f} len={}".format(L, float(best_sc), int(best_a.size)))

    # Full-length targeted search.
    final_dead = search_dead - 70.0
    final_budget = max(60.0, final_dead - time.monotonic())

    final_inits = []
    if parent is not None:
        final_inits.append(_to_len(parent, target_len))
    final_inits.append(_to_len(best_a, target_len))
    final_inits.append(_to_len(best_a[::-1], target_len))
    final_inits.extend(_structured_inits(target_len, rng, parent, cap=20))
    top_final = _rank_starts(final_inits, k=5)
    if not top_final:
        top_final = [(best_sc, _to_len(best_a, target_len))]

    if final_budget > 1200.0:
        start_arrays = []
        for r in range(min(3, len(top_final))):
            start_arrays.append(top_final[r][1])
        start_arrays.append(_perturb_a(best_a, rng, strength=0.25))
        fracs = [0.40, 0.25, 0.20, 0.15][:len(start_arrays)]
    elif final_budget > 700.0:
        start_arrays = [top_final[0][1]]
        if len(top_final) > 1:
            start_arrays.append(top_final[1][1])
        start_arrays.append(_perturb_a(best_a, rng, strength=0.30))
        fracs = [0.45, 0.30, 0.25][:len(start_arrays)]
    else:
        start_arrays = [top_final[0][1]]
        start_arrays.append(_perturb_a(best_a, rng, strength=0.35))
        fracs = [0.60, 0.40][:len(start_arrays)]

    total_frac = float(sum(fracs))
    if total_frac <= 0.0:
        fracs = [1.0 / len(start_arrays)] * len(start_arrays)
    else:
        fracs = [float(f) / total_frac for f in fracs]

    for r, start_arr in enumerate(start_arrays):
        if time.monotonic() > final_dead - 30.0:
            break
        sub_dead = time.monotonic() + final_budget * fracs[r]
        sub_dead = min(sub_dead, final_dead - 10.0)
        if time.monotonic() >= sub_dead - 10.0:
            continue

        arr_in = _to_len(start_arr, target_len)
        # Blend top secondary starts toward current best, but keep perturbations intact.
        if r != 0 and not (r == len(start_arrays) - 1 and final_budget > 500.0):
            arr_in = _normalize(0.5 * arr_in + 0.5 * _to_len(best_a, target_len))

        sc, arr2 = _optimize_start(arr_in, sub_dead, tag="final{}".format(r + 1))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = arr2.copy()
            print("final start {}: {:.8f}".format(r + 1, float(best_sc)))

    # Final high-beta Adam polish.
    high_adam_dead = search_dead - 45.0
    if time.monotonic() < high_adam_dead - 5.0:
        beta_pol = 20000.0 if best_a.size <= 4096 else 10000.0
        zpol = _logseq(best_a)
        zpol, scpol = _adam_z(
            zpol,
            beta_pol,
            high_adam_dead,
            max_step=0.15,
            lr=0.02,
            exact_every=100,
        )
        if np.isfinite(scpol) and scpol < best_sc:
            best_sc = scpol
            best_a = _softmax(zpol).copy()
            print("high-beta Adam: {:.8f}".format(float(best_sc)))

    # Final exact-guided pair moves.
    polish_dead = search_dead - 8.0
    if time.monotonic() < polish_dead - 15.0:
        scp, arrp = _pair_polish(best_a, polish_dead, rng)
        if np.isfinite(scp) and scp < best_sc:
            best_sc = scp
            best_a = arrp.copy()
            print("pair polish: {:.8f}".format(float(best_sc)))

    # Try symmetry average as a cheap final move.
    sb = best_a[::-1].copy()
    avg = _normalize(0.5 * best_a + 0.5 * sb)
    savg = _exact_fast(avg)
    if np.isfinite(savg) and sagvg < best_sc if False else (np.isfinite(savg) and savg < best_sc):
        best_sc = savg
        best_a = avg
        print("symmetry average: {:.8f}".format(float(best_sc)))

    # Prepare output.
    out_arr = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if out_arr.size == 0:
        out_arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
    out_arr = np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(out_arr.sum())
    if S < 0.01:
        out_arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        out_arr = out_arr / S

    out = [float(x) for x in out_arr]

    verified = float("nan")
    try:
        if time.monotonic() < hard_dead - 8.0:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
            if np.isfinite(verified) and verified < best_sc:
                best_sc = verified
            print(
                "FINAL internal={:.8f} verified={:.8f} len={} time={:.1f}s".format(
                    float(best_sc), float(verified), len(out), time.monotonic() - t_start
                )
            )
        else:
            print(
                "FINAL internal={:.8f} len={} time={:.1f}s (no verify time)".format(
                    float(best_sc), len(out), time.monotonic() - t_start
                )
            )
    except Exception as exc:
        print(
            "FINAL internal={:.8f} len={} time={:.1f}s verify_error={}".format(
                float(best_sc), len(out), time.monotonic() - t_start, repr(exc)
            )
        )

    return out
```
